Model before rewriting:

letting weights be [0,1,2,3,4,5;int(0..5)]
find setSize: int(1..5)
find setValues: matrix indexed by [int(1..5)] of int(0..5)
find setWeight: int(0..(sum(weights)))

such that

and([ and([(i > setSize) <-> (setValues[i] = 0),(i < setSize) -> (setValues[i] < setValues[sum([i,1;int(1..)])]);int(1..)]) | i : int(1..5) ]),
setSize > 1,
setWeight = sum([ product([catchUndef(weights[setValues[i]], 0),toInt(i <= setSize);int(1..)]) | i : int(1..5) ])
